Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Higher Order Reasoning for Collaborative Communicationless Mobile Robot Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21901)
*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态认知规划框架，使机器人在无通信环境中通过高阶信念推理实现隐式协调与长时域规划，结合贝叶斯信念更新、行为树决策和时域感知MPPI控制器，在仿真与物理实验中相比一阶基线显著缩短任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无通信环境中，多机器人系统无法依赖常规的信息交换进行协调，现有策略难以在部分可观测条件下实现高效协作，需要一种无需显式通信的隐式协调机制。

Method: 提出动态认知规划框架：机器人形成并传播高阶信念粒子，使用贝叶斯推断更新世界信念，通过行为树选择预期队友决策的动作，并采用时域感知MPPI控制器将高阶推理集成到低层轨迹执行中。

Result: 在仿真与物理实验中，所提框架相比一阶基线方法一致性地减少了任务完成时间，验证了认知逻辑在通信受限领域作为鲁棒协调基础的有效性。

Conclusion: 认知逻辑可作为无通信环境下多机器人弹性协调的可靠基础，高阶信念推理能有效替代显式通信实现隐式协作。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>


### [184] [Non-Contact Vibration-Based Damage Detection of Civil Structures Using a Cost-Effective Autonomous UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21914)
*Javier Becerril,Maximiliano Vargas,Jennifer Herrera,Joanna Gutierrez,Jorge Rios,Mohsen Amjadian,Constantine Tarawneh,Jinghao Yang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低成本自主无人机（UAV）的非接触式振动结构损伤检测方法，通过视频运动跟踪提取振动信号识别固有频率变化。实验验证了多种低成本平台（智能手机、USB摄像头、定制无人机）均能可靠检测损伤引起的频率偏移，无人机虽存在最高5.7%的误差但可接受，为结构健康监测提供了实用、灵活、可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式传感器在结构健康监测中存在安装成本高、维护困难、难以覆盖危险或难以到达区域等局限性，需要一种低成本、非接触、可自主部署的替代方案来实现结构损伤的远程检测。

Method: 采用自主定制的低成本无人机搭载机载摄像头，通过基于视觉的运动跟踪从视频记录中提取振动信号，在频域分析中识别结构固有频率的变化；同时集成自主对准系统以在无GPS环境下运行。实验使用智能手机、USB摄像头和定制无人机三种低成本平台对实验室框架结构在健康与模拟损伤条件下进行测试，并与接触式加速度计及有限元模型结果对比验证。

Result: 所有低成本平台均成功捕获了结构基频及其损伤引起的频率偏移。无人机平台因平台扰动和传感限制，误差略高（最高5.7%），但仍能可靠检测损伤导致的频率变化；相比于商用无人机系统，该平台在显著降低成本的同时实现了相当的检测性能。

Conclusion: 低成本自主无人机为非接触式结构健康监测提供了一种实用、灵活且可扩展的方案，特别适用于接触式传感不可行的场景；研究结果还支持未来部署多架协作无人机以进一步提升检测覆盖范围和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation. A laboratory-scale frame structure is evaluated under healthy and simulated-damage conditions. The proposed system is validated through an experimental study involving two smartphones, a USB camera, and a custom-built low-cost UAV equipped with an onboard camera and an autonomous alignment system for operation in GPS-denied environments. The displacement time is extracted and analyzed in the frequency domain and compared to reference measurements from contact accelerometers and a finite element model. Experimental results show that all platforms successfully capture the fundamental frequency and its shift due to damage. Although the UAV exhibits slightly higher errors (up to 5.7%) due to platform-induced disturbances and sensing limitations, it reliably detects damage-induced frequency changes. Compared to commercial UAV systems, the proposed platform achieves comparable inspection performance at significantly lower cost. These results demonstrate that low-cost autonomous UAVs provide a practical, flexible, and scalable solution for structural health monitoring, particularly in scenarios where contact-based sensing is impractical. The findings also support the potential for the deployment of multiple cooperative UAVs to further enhance inspection coverage and robustness.

</details>


### [185] [Learning to Evolve: Multi-modal Interactive Fields for Robust Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21935)
*Peifeng Jiang,Hong Liu,Jin Jin,Wenshuai Wang,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态交互场(MIF)系统，通过置信度感知的语义3D高斯泼溅、差异触发的空间记忆更新和任务驱动的几何重建，实现仿人机器人在动态环境下的安全操控导航，在真实机器人上将重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，内存占用降低91.4%。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图和场景图系统因假设稳定相机轨迹、静态环境或粗糙物体几何，难以直接部署在受运动感知畸变、环境变化和交互级几何安全约束影响的仿人机器人操控导航场景中。 

Method: 提出MIF系统，包含三个场：不确定性感知的3DGS外观场(抑制步态模糊)、空间场(维护拓扑记忆)和几何场(支持操控前交互姿势安全)；采用差异检测分数区分步态引起的假阳性变化与持久性变化，仅更新局部不一致区域；通过特征蒸馏压缩语义内存。 

Result: 在Unitree-G1仿人机器人真实动态办公室环境中，非静态环境下重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，语义内存占用降低91.4%。 

Conclusion: MIF通过闭环比对感知-适应流程，有效解决了仿人机器人在动态环境中因 locomotion 导致的感知畸变和场景变化问题，实现了高可靠性的操控导航。 

Abstract: Safe manipulation-oriented navigation for humanoid robots requires scene memory that remains reliable under locomotion-induced perceptual distortion, environmental changes, and interaction-level geometric safety constraints. Existing semantic mapping and scene-graph systems are difficult to deploy directly in this setting because they often assume stable camera trajectories, static environments, or coarse object geometry. We introduce the Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF), a humanoid-oriented system that integrates confidence-aware semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting, discrepancy-triggered spatial memory updates, and task-driven geometric reconstruction within a closed-loop perception-adaptation pipeline. MIF couples three fields: an uncertainty-aware 3DGS Appearance Field that suppresses gait-induced blur, a Spatial Field that maintains topological memory, and a Geometry Field that supports Interaction Pose Safety (IPS) before manipulation. A discrepancy detection score is introduced to separate locomotion-induced false-positive changes from persistent changes and updates only locally inconsistent regions. On a Unitree-G1 humanoid in a real dynamic office, MIF improves relocation success in non-static environments from 12% to 94% compared with static scene-graph memory, while reducing semantic memory footprint by 91.4% through feature distillation for practical online operation. Project page and code: https://ziya-jiang.github.io/MIF-homepage/

</details>


### [186] [A Visitation Grid for Complete Coverage Foraging in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21947)
*Qi Arturo Gonzalez,Yifeng Gao,Li Zhang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于网格的随机群智能体觅食策略，通过划分未知环境为网格并由轻量级中央服务器维护访问计数，引导机器人概率性地选择访问次数最少的邻域进行搜索，从而减少冗余访问、加速末期收集。仿真实验表明，该方法相比CPFA基线算法，总收集时间减少33%，末期收集效率提升48%以上，具备鲁棒性、灵活性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体觅食算法在收集大部分资源后，末端阶段（仅剩少量随机分散资源）消耗大量任务时间，且现有研究多关注有限时间窗口内收集大部分资源，而非提升末端效率以完成完全收集。

Method: 将未知搜索区域划分为网格地图，由轻量级中央服务器维护网格级访问计数；机器人每次觅食时从当前所在网格的3×3局部邻域中，基于访问计数概率性地选择访问次数最低的网格作为下一搜索区域，在保持随机性的同时偏向未充分探索区域。

Result: 在大量仿真实验中，该方法相比规范的中心放置基线觅食算法（CPFA），总收集时间降低33%，末端收集效率提升48%以上。

Conclusion: 所提策略鲁棒、灵活且可扩展，适用于机器人群体近乎完全和完全的资源收集任务，可作为在有限机载资源下随机群体觅食方法的一般性增强方案。

Abstract: The complete collection of sparse resources in large, unknown environments remains a challenging problem for autonomous robot swarms. Previous studies have shown that a substantial portion of total mission time is consumed during the final stage of collection, where only a small fraction of randomly scattered resources remain. Consequently, many existing swarm foraging algorithms (search and collection) focus on collecting most resources within a limited time window, rather than improving end-stage efficiency for collecting all resources. We propose a grid-based stochastic foraging strategy that explicitly reduces redundant visits and accelerates late-stage collection. The unknown search area is partitioned into a grid map, which is maintained by a lightweight central server. To maintain scalability, both robots and the server operate within limited memory and computational constraints. The server updates the grid-level visitation counts based on robot-reported locations, producing a global estimate of the exploration density. For each new foraging trip, a robot selects its next search area from a local 3 X 3 neighborhood of grids probabilistically with the lowest visitation count, thus biasing exploration toward under-visited regions while maintaining stochasticity. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently outperforms the canonical centrally placed baseline foraging algorithm (CPFA). Compared to CPFA, the proposed method reduces the total collection time by up to 33% and improves collection efficiency by more than 48% during the final stage of the mission. These results indicate that the proposed strategy is robust, flexible, and scalable for near-complete and complete resource collection in robot swarms and can serve as a general enhancement for stochastic swarm foraging methods under limited onboard resources.

</details>


### [187] [TacO: Benchmarking Tactile Sensors for Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21976)
*Anya Zorin,Zilin Si,Myungsun Park,Junsung Park,Alexiy Buynitsky,Sachin Bhadang,Taejun Park,Sohee John Yoon,Yong-Lae Park,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel,Michael T. Tolley,Sha Yi,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种触觉传感器模态（视觉、声学、磁学、电阻式）在三种机器人操作任务（未知质量抓取、物体重定向、插头插入）中的表现，发现触觉传感器的有效性高度依赖于传感器模态、材料属性和具体任务，而非普遍适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管触觉感知被广泛认为能提升机器人操作性能，但缺乏系统性的经验指导来确定哪种触觉传感器最适合哪种操作任务。

Method: 针对四种不同模态的触觉传感器（视觉、声学、磁学、电阻式），分别在三个任务（未知质量抓取、物体重定向、插头插入）中训练独立的操作策略，分析传感器属性（空间分辨率、剪切力感知、触觉表征）和材料摩擦对任务性能的影响。

Result: 触觉信息的有用性并非普遍一致，而是强烈依赖于传感器模态、材料属性和具体操作任务。

Conclusion: 提出了基于操作策略性能的触觉传感器选择与评估框架，为不同操作任务选择最合适的触觉传感器提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Vision-based learning from demonstrations has achieved remarkable success in enabling robots to perform manipulation tasks and high-level semantic reasoning, yet it remains insufficient for complex, contact-rich manipulation. While there is broad agreement that tactile sensing improves manipulation, there is no empirical guidance on which tactile sensors are best suited for which manipulation tasks. In this paper, we provide a systematic, task-driven evaluation of tactile sensors for robot manipulation and propose a framework for selecting and evaluating sensors based on manipulation policy performance. Separate manipulation policies are trained for tactile sensors of four distinct modalities: visual, acoustic, magnetic, and resistive, across three tasks: pick-and-place with unknown mass, object reorientation, and plug insertion. For each task, an analysis of how sensor properties such as spatial resolution, shear sensing, and tactile representation, and the inherent material friction affect task performances is done. Rather than tactile sensing being universally beneficial in the same way, our results show that the usefulness of tactile information depends strongly on sensor modality, material properties, and the specific manipulation tasks. All of the tactile sensors, code, data, and hardware setup will be publicly available on the project website.

</details>


### [188] [Industrial Dual-Arm Box Handling via Online Inertial Estimation and Convex Wrench Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22021)
*Kenzhi Iskandar Wong,Lin Yang,Qian Ying Lee,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种摩擦感知的双臂箱体操作框架，通过在线估计未知惯性参数（质量与质心），利用椭球摩擦极限曲面约束下的二阶锥规划计算可行接触力与扭转力矩，并辅以离线轨迹优化，实现了对未知惯性属性物体的稳定抓取，避免了滑移与过度挤压问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人操作箱体时，物体的质量和质心通常未知，这些不确定性影响提升过程中的力-力矩平衡，不当的接触力调节会导致滑移、物体掉落、姿态偏差或过度挤压。

Method: 在线从测量的接触力中估计物体质量和质心；通过二阶锥规划（SOCP）在椭球摩擦极限曲面约束下计算摩擦可行的接触力和扭转力矩；包含离线轨迹优化阶段以减少不必要的物体-环境接触。

Result: 在真实双臂机器人系统上，针对不同质心配置的实验表明，该方法能够成功提升未知惯性属性的物体，同时保持稳定的摩擦接触。

Conclusion: 通过将摩擦可行性作为硬约束并在可行区域内最小化接触力，该框架在不将滑移避免与过度挤压作为分离调节目标的前提下实现了稳定提升。

Abstract: Industrial robotic object handling often involves boxes and packages whose mass and center of mass are not known in advance. These uncertainties affect the force--moment balance required for stable lifting, and improper regulation of contact wrenches can lead to slip, object drop, orientation deviation, or excessive squeezing. This paper presents a friction-aware dual-arm box-handling framework for objects with unknown inertial properties. The proposed approach estimates the object mass and center of mass online from measured contact wrenches, and computes friction-feasible contact forces and torsional moments through a second-order cone program (SOCP) under ellipsoidal friction-limit-surface constraints. An offline trajectory refinement stage is also included to reduce undesired object--environment contact when geometric constraints are present. By enforcing friction feasibility as a hard constraint and minimizing contact effort within the feasible region, the framework achieves stable lifting without treating slip avoidance and excessive squeezing as separately tuned objectives. Experiments on a real dual-arm robotic system under different center-of-mass configurations demonstrate that the method lifts objects with unknown inertial properties while maintaining stable frictional contact.

</details>


### [189] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA是一个基于对比学习的机器人运动自适应框架，通过6D语义接触上下文替代原始模拟器参数，结合因果Transformer适配器和力域对比目标，在无需演示、特权信息或梯度更新的情况下实现装配任务内的在线自适应，在PegInsert、GearMesh、NutThread任务及真实Marvin机械臂上验证了相比FORGE基线更高的真实成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMA方法依赖原始模拟器参数适配，在力主导的装配任务中表现不佳；FORGE等高仿真成功率方法在真实硬件上性能大幅下降，亟需一种可跨任务复用的、无需梯度更新或特权信息的在线自适应机制。

Method: 提出CoRMA框架：1) 用紧凑的6D模拟器专属语义接触上下文（接触起始、侧向啮合、引导过渡、接触方向、卡滞）替代原始参数适配；2) 部署时使用因果Transformer适配器，基于力、本体感知和动作历史，通过语义回归和力域对比目标在线推断该上下文；3) 运行时移除oracle上下文，替换为推断上下文，实现无需演示、特权输入或梯度更新的episode内自适应。

Result: 在PegInsert、GearMesh、NutThread任务及Isaac Lab/Isaac Sim 5.0仿真和真实Marvin机械臂上评估。相比FORGE基线（高仿真成功但硬件上大幅退化），CoRMA在受控目标位姿噪声下保持了更高的真实验证成功率。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断可作为相关装配任务家族内可复用的自适应接口；更广泛的未见任务泛化与Real2Sim标定仍为未来工作。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim~5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Pixels: Learning Invariant Rewards for Real-World Robotics From a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22123)
*Tengye Xu,Yangting Sun,Ziju Shen,Guanqi Chen,Zhen Fu,Chen yizhou,Hua Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从少量演示中学习不变符号奖励函数的框架，通过发现行为不变量而非拟合视觉特征，实现奖励在位置、视角和物体变化下的零样本泛化，并在Meta-World和Franka操作任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的奖励模型倾向于记忆特定像素分布，在开放世界操作问题中难以泛化到训练条件之外的物体实例、位置和相机视角变化。

Method: 提出包含两个耦合组件的框架：1）结构化的奖励公式化，编码任务级策略和物理约束，同时保持最优策略不变性；2）混合符号-数值程序，从演示中蒸馏这些不变量，无需在线交互。

Result: 在8个Meta-World任务和3个Franka操作任务上，该方法在过程对齐和策略 rollout 排序能力上优于基线，并加速了下游策略学习。三个真实世界的分布外实验表明，同一奖励可零样本泛化到位置、视角和物体变化。

Conclusion: 该方法通过学习符号化行为不变量而非视觉特征，使得单个奖励表示可跨多样的任务变体重用，在实际应用中具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Designing reward functions that generalize beyond controlled laboratory settings remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning for robotics. In open-world manipulation problems, a single task can appear in numerous variants through different object instances, positions, and camera viewpoints. Recent vision-based reward models tend to memorize specific pixel distributions and fail to generalize beyond their training conditions. To address this, we propose a framework that learns invariant symbolic reward functions from as few as five demonstrations. The insight is to shift from visual feature-fitting to the discovery of behavioral invariants: task-level properties that remain constant across diverse visual instantiations. The framework has two coupled components: a structural reward formulation that encodes task-level strategies and physical constraints while preserving optimal policy invariance, and a hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure that distills these invariants from demonstrations without online interaction. Experiments on eight Meta-World tasks and three Franka manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method achieves stronger process alignment and policy rollout ranking abilities compared to baselines, accelerating downstream policy learning. Three real-world out-of-distribution experiments further show that the same learned reward generalizes zero-shot to position, viewpoint, and object variations, enabling a single reward representation to be reused across diverse task variants in practice.

</details>


### [191] [Action with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22183)
*Weilong Guo,Yuchen Wang,Renping Zhou,Yunfeng Zhang,Rui Fang,Yue Meng,Wenda Xu,Yuan He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVP是一种端到端的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)架构，通过让VLM推断下一阶段目标并生成视觉基元令牌来条件化流匹配动作专家，从而解耦认知感知与运动控制，在通用拾取放置任务上比pi_0.5提升27.61%的成功率，并在数据效率、空间组合泛化和物体级迁移方面取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将语言指令理解、空间场景感知和运动控制融合在单一学习目标中，导致动作专家必须隐式重新学习预训练VLM中已有的认知和感知能力，限制了学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出AVP架构，其核心是视觉基元为中心的接口：VLM推断下一阶段目标并发射视觉基元令牌，用于条件化一个流匹配动作专家，动作专家的监督信号来自末端执行器运动学。

Result: 在通用拾取放置任务的真实机器人实验中，AVP相比pi_0.5提升27.61%的成功率，并优于其他近期方法；在数据效率、空间-组合泛化和物体级迁移方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过解耦高层认知推理（VLM负责）与底层动作生成（流匹配专家负责），AVP有效提升了VLA模型的学习效率和泛化能力，验证了视觉基元接口设计的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. A common design in current architectures maps language instructions and visual observations to actions in a single forward pass. While conceptually simple, this formulation entangles instruction comprehension, spatial scene understanding, and motor control within a single learning objective. As a result, the action expert must implicitly relearn cognitive and perceptual capabilities already present in the pretrained VLM, which can limit both learning efficiency and generalization. We introduce AVP (Action with Visual Primitives), an end-to-end architecture that implements this visual-primitive-centric interface: the VLM infers the next-stage target and emits visual-primitive tokens that condition a flow-matching action expert, with supervision derived from end-effector kinematics. Real-robot experiments on general pick-and-place tasks show that AVP improves the success rate by 27.61% over pi_0.5 and outperforms other recent methods, with consistent gains in data efficiency, spatial-compositional generalization, and object-level transfer.

</details>


### [192] [Learning A Unified Risk Map for Autonomous Driving in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22189)
*Jie Jia,Yaofeng Su,Zeyu Bao,Yun Hong,Bingzhao Gao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向部分可观测环境的统一风险图建模与学习框架，通过整合交通流风险与碰撞风险实现遮挡感知预测，并引入扩散模型生成对抗性遮挡场景，在Waymo数据集上显著提升了时间至碰撞指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遮挡感知方法要么基于可达状态过度估计风险，要么在高遮挡不确定性下难以预测准确轨迹，缺乏对遮挡诱导风险的细粒度评估能力。

Method: 提出统一风险图框架：1) 通过时空建模整合交通流风险与碰撞风险；2) 引入扩散模型生成真实且对抗性的遮挡交互场景；3) 将风险图建模与学习集成到部分可观测条件下的风险感知规划中。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，与最先进遮挡感知基线相比，最小时间至碰撞提升0.78倍，平均时间至碰撞提升1.67倍。

Conclusion: 所提框架为部分可观测环境下的风险感知规划提供了全面且实用的解决方案，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Occlusion-aware prediction remains a critical challenge in autonomous driving due to the inherent uncertainty of unobserved regions. Existing approaches either overestimate risk based on reachable states or struggle to predict accurate trajectories under high occlusion uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified risk map modeling and learning framework for partially observable environments. Our method integrates traffic flow risk and collision risk through spatiotemporal modeling, enabling fine-grained assessment of occlusion-induced hazards. To address the scarcity of scenarios involving occluded interactions, we introduce a diffusion-based scenario generation framework that produces realistic yet adversarial scenarios. We integrate the modeling and learning of a unified risk map into a framework that supports risk-aware planning under partial observability. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art occlusion-aware baseline, improving minimum time-to-collision by 0.78 times and average time-to-collision by 1.67 times. The proposed framework offers a comprehensive and practical solution for risk-aware planning in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [193] [Imagine2Real: Towards Zero-shot Humanoid-Object Interaction via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22272)
*Jiahe Chen,ZiRui Wang,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Xiaojie Niu,Weishuai Zeng,Tianfan Xue,Xiaowei Zhou,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine2Real是一个零样本人形机器人-物体交互框架，通过将机器人和物体运动统一表示为4D点轨迹，并仅跟踪稀疏的关键点（基座、手和物体），绕过重定向过程，利用行为基础模型的潜在空间作为搜索域，实现无需几何先验的灵活交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形机器人-物体交互（HOI）受到高质量3D数据稀缺的瓶颈制约。现有方法依赖几何先验（如显式CAD模型）导致表示错位，且需要密集变形和形态匹配，重定向复杂。

Method: 1) 将机器人和物体运动统一表示为4D点轨迹，解决表示错位；2) 提出关键点追踪器（Keypoints Tracker），仅追踪基座、手和物体的稀疏关键点，完全绕过重定向过程；3) 利用行为基础模型（BFM）的潜在空间作为追踪器的搜索域，以在稀疏信号下保持自然步态；4) 采用渐进式训练策略，通过简单的追踪奖励学习鲁棒行为。

Result: 实现了零样本物理部署，可在运动捕捉系统中运行。

Conclusion: Imagine2Real通过统一的4D点轨迹表示和稀疏关键点追踪，有效解决了表示错位和重定向复杂性问题，为零样本人形机器人-物体交互提供了灵活、无需几何先验的解决方案。

Abstract: Whole-body Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data. While video generative priors offer a promising alternative, existing methods suffer from \textit{Representation Misalignment} due to their reliance on geometric priors (e.g., explicit CAD models), and \textit{Retargeting Complexity} arising from intensive morphing and morphological mismatch. We propose Imagine2Real, a zero-shot HOI framework for flexible, geometry-free interaction. To resolve misalignment, we formulate robot and object motions as unified 4D point trajectories. To overcome retargeting complexity, our Keypoints Tracker tracks only sparse critical points (base, hands, and object), entirely bypassing the error-amplifying retargeting process. To maintain natural gaits despite these sparse signals, we utilize the latent space of a Behavior Foundation Model (BFM) as the tracker's search domain. Using a progressive training strategy, Imagine2Real learns robust behaviors with simple tracking rewards, enabling zero-shot physical deployment within a motion capture(mocap) system.

</details>


### [194] [Spatial Memory for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22283)
*Pengteng Li,Weiyu Guo,He Zhang,Tiefu Cai,Xiao He,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOMA是一个为视觉-语言-动作模型设计的空间记忆框架，通过多视角观测构建持久空间记忆，使机器人能够在目标物体脱离视野时仍能完成操作任务，在五项真实世界任务中显著提升了成功率并实现了接近单次抓取的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型隐式假设任务相关物体始终可见，当目标超出相机视野时会产生脆弱和被动的行为，无法处理视线之外的物体操作问题。

Method: SOMA包含三个核心组件：1) 空间记忆构建(Spatial Memory Construction)：通过可移动头部相机扫描多视角观测，聚合为统一的空间-语义表示；2) 动态记忆细化(Dynamic Memory Refinement)：随时间维护全局一致性；3) 上下文记忆检索(Contextual Memory Retrieval)：在操作过程中激活与指令相关的空间线索。

Result: 在五项挑战性的真实世界视觉外操作任务中，SOMA不仅提高了任务成功率，还产生了定性不同的操作行为：更快的目标定位、更少的视角搜索、在部分可观测条件下实现接近单次抓取。在RoboCasa GR1和SimplerEnv的完全可观测场景中也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: SOMA通过引入空间记忆机制有效解决了VLA模型在目标脱离视野时的操作问题，使机器人能够进行超越当前视觉范围的推理，从而在部分可观测条件下表现出更智能和高效的操作行为。

Abstract: We introduce SOMA, the Spatial Memory framework for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Most existing VLAs implicitly assume that task-relevant objects are always visible, leading to brittle and reactive behaviors when targets fall outside the camera's field of view. SOMA addresses this limitation by equipping VLAs with a persistent spatial memory constructed from multi-view observations acquired via a movable head camera, enabling reasoning beyond the current visual frustum. The framework consists of three components: Spatial Memory Construction, which aggregates angular-wise observations into a unified spatial-semantic representation through scanning; Dynamic Memory Refinement, which maintains global consistency over time; and Contextual Memory Retrieval, which activates instruction-relevant spatial cues during manipulation. We evaluate SOMA on five challenging real-world out-of-vision manipulation tasks, including multi-step and dual-arm scenarios where target objects are initially invisible. Experimental results show that SOMA not only improves task success rates, but also induces qualitatively different manipulation behaviors, with faster target localization, reduced viewpoint search, and near one-shot grasping under partial observability. Additional experiments on RoboCasa GR1 and SimplerEnv further validate the effectiveness of SOMA's memory design under conventional fully observable settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [195] [How can reasoning capability empower the AI copilot robot in endoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22322)
*Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了推理能力在基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的AI副手手术机器人中的应用潜力，提出通过多模态线索整合、手术意图理解及组织动态推理，将机器人从被动执行者转变为认知协作者，以提升内镜手术的精准性、安全性和可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理能力已在通用领域的复杂逻辑推理和机器人决策中取得显著进展，但在基于VLA模型的内镜手术AI副手机器人中尚未被探索。现有手术机器人多为被动执行工具，缺乏对多模态信息整合、手术意图理解及隐藏组织动态推理的能力，导致术中不确定性高、认知负担重。

Method: 提出将推理能力引入VLA模型框架，使AI副手机器人能够整合视觉、语言等多模态线索，理解手术意图，并推断隐藏的组织动态信息，从而实现从被动执行到认知协作的转变。

Result: 推理驱动的自主性可使AI副手机器人从反应式执行器转变为认知协作者，提升内镜手术的精准性、安全性和可持续性。

Conclusion: 推理能力的引入有望变革AI副手手术机器人的角色定位，通过认知协作减轻术中不确定性及外科医生的认知负担，推动临床实践向更精准、更安全、更可持续的方向发展。

Abstract: Reasoning capability has significantly advanced complex logical inference and robotic decision-making in general domains. However, its potential in the Artificial Intelligence (AI) copilot robot-particularly implemented based on the Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model-remains unexplored in endoscopic surgery. Effective reasoning should enable AI copilot robots to integrate multimodal cues, interpret surgical intent, and infer hidden tissue dynamics, thereby alleviating intraoperative uncertainty and cognitive burden on surgeons. Properly implemented, reasoning-driven autonomy can transform AI copilot robots from reactive executors into cognitive collaborators, enhancing precision, safety, and sustainability in clinical practice.

</details>


### [196] [Real-Time Auto-Optimization in Unknown Environments via Structure-Exploiting Dual Control for Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22431)
*Shiying Dong,Haoyang Yang,Qiwei Liu,Wen-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速数值双控探索-利用方法，通过利用DCEE奖励函数固有的凸-非线性结构，将子问题转化为结构化凸形式，在车辆巡航自动优化问题上实现约一个数量级的加速和微秒级计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCEE方法虽然提供了原则性的探索-利用目标，但主要依赖标准优化包或显式梯度型更新律实现，未能充分利用DCEE的数值结构，导致计算负担成为主动学习中的主要问题。

Method: 揭示了DCEE中奖励函数具有固有的凸-非线性结构（凸外层损失包裹非线性残差映射），仅线性化非线性残差映射而保留凸外层损失，将每个子问题转化为可可靠求解的结构化凸形式，所得广义高斯-牛顿黑塞矩阵正半定且仅依赖一阶导数。

Result: 在车辆巡航自动优化问题上，所提方法相比现有方法提升了控制性能，实现了约一个数量级的加速，在典型车辆嵌入式CPU上最大计算时间仅83微秒。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构利用数值方法有效解决了DCEE的计算瓶颈，兼具理论保证与实时性，适用于未知环境下的自动优化问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a fast numerical dual control for exploration and exploitation (DCEE) method to address auto-optimization problems in unknown environments. In auto-optimization problems, the optimal operating condition is unknown a priori and may vary with the environment. As in classical dual control techniques, computational burden remains a major concern in DCEE for active learning. Existing DCEE methods provide a principled exploration-exploitation objective, but mainly realized through standard optimization packages or explicit gradient-type update laws, where the numerical structure of the DCEE has not been fully exploited. This paper shows that the reward function in DCEE has an inherent convex-over-nonlinear structure, where the exploitation and exploration terms form a unified nonlinear residual map equipped with a convex outer loss. Benefiting from this structure, a structure-exploiting numerical method is developed by linearizing only the nonlinear residual map while preserving the convex outer loss. Thus, each subproblem is transformed into a structured convex form that can be solved reliably. The resulting generalized Gauss-Newton Hessian approximation is positive semidefinite and depends only on first-order derivatives, thereby supporting fast online computation. The proposed method is evaluated on a vehicle cruising auto-optimization problem and compared with existing methods. Simulation and hardware-in-the-loop experimental results show that the proposed method improves control performance and achieves a speedup of approximately one order of magnitude, with a microsecond-level maximum computation time of only 83 μs on a typical vehicle embedded CPU.

</details>


### [197] [Terminal Constraint Model Predictive Control for Image-Based Visual Servoing of UAVs with Kalman Filter-Based Moment Loss Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22443)
*X. Wang,Y. Cao,W. L. W. Leong,Y. R. Tan,S. Huang,S. H. R. Teo,C. Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于图像的视觉伺服（IBVS）在无人机应用中存在的近目标稳定性丧失和视觉特征间歇性丢失导致控制失败的问题，提出了一种集成卡尔曼滤波状态预测的终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）框架，通过终端状态约束和终端代价函数确保递归可行性和闭环稳定性，利用卡尔曼滤波在视觉退化期间预测图像矩演化以维持控制连续性，并通过实时无人机视觉伺服实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBVS控制器在无人机视觉伺服中存在两个关键问题：一是近目标时输入和状态约束导致闭环稳定性丧失，二是剧烈运动下基于矩的视觉特征间歇性丢失导致控制失败。

Method: 提出了终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）框架，显式引入终端状态约束和终端代价函数到IBVS误差动力学中，同时集成卡尔曼滤波器（KF）预测图像矩的时间演化以应对视觉短暂退化。

Result: 通过实时无人机视觉伺服实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TC-MPC结合KF预测机制能够同时解决IBVS中的稳定性丧失和视觉特征丢失问题，确保递归可行性和控制连续性。

Abstract: Image-Based Visual Servoing (IBVS) provides an efficient vision-guided control paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) by directly regulating image-space errors. However, conventional IBVS controllers are vulnerable to two critical issues: loss of closed-loop stability near the target due to input and state constraints, and control failure caused by intermittent loss of moment-based visual features under aggressive motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a terminal-constraint model predictive control (TC-MPC) framework for IBVS, integrated with a Kalman filter (KF)-based state-prediction mechanism. The TC-MPC explicitly incorporates terminal-state constraints and a terminal cost into the IBVS error dynamics, ensuring recursive feasibility, improved convergence behavior, and closed-loop stability under control and state constraints. In parallel, the Kalman filter predicts the temporal evolution of image moments during short-term visual degradation, enabling the controller to preserve control continuity when moment measurements are partially unavailable. The proposed approach is validated through real-time UAV visual servoing experiments.

</details>


### [198] [Steins;Gate Drive: Semantic Safety Arbitration over Structured Futures for Latency-Decoupled LLM Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22456)
*Anjie Qiu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SteinsGateDrive提出了一种延迟解耦的规划器-运行时架构，让LLM在最终控制时刻之前选择反事实驾驶未来，运行时仅在安全合约有效时复用所选预测。实验表明，GPT-5.4 mini在4秒预测时延下将有效延迟从+3.07秒降至-0.01秒，同时保持无碰撞安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管LLM驱动的智能体提供有用的语义判断，但其推理延迟超过了步进式车辆控制的时间窗口。现有学习型世界模型虽然能预测未来，但通常将未来生成和动作选择耦合在大型循环中，导致延迟问题。

Method: 提出延迟解耦的规划器-运行时架构，基于"世界线"隐喻构建三类世界线角色：alpha标称自我条件未来、beta附近车辆交互反事实、gamma危险应力未来（如刹车、切入、阻塞车道）。选中的分支成为带时间范围、有效性/中止条件、回退和权限的带类型StrategicForecast。运行时仅在原子谓词安全合约有效时复用所选预测。

Result: 在受试者内、匹配种子的正常公路协议上（10种子、20步），GPT-5.4 mini将有效延迟从1秒预测范围下的+3.07秒降低到4秒预测范围下的-0.01秒，同时保持测量的无碰撞安全边界。

Conclusion: 架构的安全贡献来自原子谓词运行时检查，而非漂移评分——漂移评分仅作为刷新频率旋钮。该方案有效解耦了LLM推理延迟与车辆控制的时间窗口要求。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted LLM driver agents provide useful semantic judgments, but their inference latency exceeds stepwise vehicle-control windows. Learned world models predict futures, but they usually keep future generation and action selection inside large coupled loops. We present SteinsGateDrive, a latency-decoupled planner-runtime architecture in which the worldline metaphor from the eponymous story names one plausible consequence of an intervention: the LLM selects counterfactual driving futures before the final control instant, and a runtime reuses the selected forecast only while safety contracts remain valid. The generator builds three world-line roles: alpha nominal ego-conditioned futures, beta interaction counterfactuals around nearby vehicles, and gamma hazard-stress futures such as braking, cut-ins, or blocked corridors. The selected branch becomes a typed StrategicForecast with horizon, validity/abort conditions, fallback, and authority. On a within-subject, matched-seed normal-highway protocol with 10 seeds and 20 steps, GPT-5.4 mini reduces effective lag from +3.07 s at 1-second horizon to -0.01 s at 4-second horizon while preserving the measured no-collision safety boundary. The architecture's safety contribution comes from the atom-predicate runtime check, not from the drift score, which functions as a refresh-frequency knob.

</details>


### [199] [Quantifying Full-Body Immersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22521)
*Alihan Bakir,Ekrem Yüksel,Fabio Zuliani,Neil Chennoufi,Francesco Bruno,Jamie Paik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模块化机器人表面的全身动力学交互沉浸式虚拟体验范式，通过三级沉浸（视听、物理、全身）实现真实与虚拟世界的融合，并构建了可扩展、可分布的平台，支持从个人到多用户场景的动态物理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前虚拟现实体验在全身交互与物理反馈方面存在局限，真实与虚拟世界之间的界限尚未充分消解，亟需一种能够实现全身动力学交互的沉浸式平台，以弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出三级沉浸框架（视听沉浸、物理沉浸、全身沉浸FBI），核心是受自然界适应性设计启发的模块化机器人表面单元，这些单元可动态分布并实时响应交互，在空间中分布力、形状和运动反馈，实现任意规模的沉浸环境渲染。

Result: 实现了可扩展、可分布的模块化平台，能够从个人体验到多用户大空间场景动态适配；通过全身沉浸（FBI）实现了用户与虚拟空间的全身共生交互，提供了前所未有的物理参与深度。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅推动了沉浸式技术的发展，更重要的是重新定义了人类与虚拟环境共存的方式，为人机环境融合的新时代奠定了基础。

Abstract: Humanity is at the forefront of yet another digital revolution, where the lines between real and virtual worlds are dissolving, reshaping how we perceive and interact with our surroundings. In this context, we introduce a transformative paradigm for immersive virtual experiences centered around whole-body kinetic interactions. Our approach redefines immersion through three distinct levels: audio-visual immersion, capturing sensory realism; physical immersion, delivering haptic feedback; and full-body immersion (FBI), where dynamic bodily interaction integrates seamlessly with virtual environments. At the core of this innovation lies a scalable, distributable platform based on modular robotic surface units inspired by the adaptive designs of nature. These units enable the rendering of immersive environments at any scale, from intimate personal experiences to expansive multi-user settings, dynamically adapting to interactions in real-time. The modular system distributes force, shape, and motion feedback throughout entire spaces, replicating the physical characteristics of the environment and enabling new depth of engagement through FBI. By combining scalability, adaptability, and dynamic physical engagement, this framework bridges the gap between real and virtual worlds. It offers an unprecedented level of immersion where users can engage their entire bodies in symbiotic interactions with the virtual space. This work not only advances immersive technology but also redefines how humans and virtual environments coexist, setting a foundation for a new era of human-environment synthesis.

</details>


### [200] [SE3Kit: A Lightweight Python Library for Specialized Geometric Primitives in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22633)
*Daniyal Maroufi,Omid Rezayof,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SE3Kit是一个轻量级、纯Python实现的李群运算库，专注于SE(3)和SO(3)的严格数学运算，填补了现有库在轻量性与数学严谨性之间的空白，适用于嵌入式部署、快速原型开发和教学场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python机器人学领域缺乏既轻量级又在数学上严谨的刚体变换库，现有方案要么依赖繁重的库（如SpatialMath、PyPose），要么缺乏机器人学专用功能（如SciPy）。

Method: 采用纯Python和NumPy实现李群运算，无深度学习或其他可视化软件的开销，专注于SE(3)和SO(3)群上的高效运算。

Result: 构建了一个轻量级的、数学上严格的SE(3)和SO(3)运算库，支持嵌入式部署、快速原型开发和教学。

Conclusion: SE3Kit成功填补了现有刚体变换库在轻量性与数学严谨性之间的空白，为机器人学社区提供了一个无冗余依赖的高效工具。

Abstract: The Python robotics ecosystem faces a challenge: while many libraries exist for rigid body transformations, few are both lightweight and mathematically strict. This paper introduces SE3Kit, a lightweight Python library efficient operations on the Special Euclidean Group SE(3) and the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3). Unlike established frameworks that require heavy dependencies (e.g., SpatialMath, PyPose) or general tools that lack robotics-specific features (e.g., SciPy), SE3Kit targets the gap between these extremes. It is designed for embedded deployment, rapid prototyping, and education while providing rigorous mathematical implementation. It provides a pure-Python, NumPy-only implementation of Lie Group operations, without the overhead of deep learning or other visualization software.

</details>


### [201] [Symmetries Here and There, Combined Everywhere: Cross-space Symmetry Compositions in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22639)
*Loizos Hadjiloizou,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出跨空间对称性组合框架，通过前向运动学的微分几何结构实现配置空间与任务空间多重对称性的联合等变，在双机械臂实验上验证了改进的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统存在来自机械结构和任务属性的丰富对称性，但现有方法通常孤立处理这些对称性，未能利用其组合潜力。

Method: 利用前向运动学映射的微分几何结构，将对称性从配置空间降维到任务空间，同时将对称性从任务空间提升到配置空间，在统一表示空间内实现多重对称性的组合。

Result: 在双机械臂的仿真和真实实验中，联合利用多重对称性相比基线方法展现出更优的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 跨空间对称性组合框架能够有效融合多重对称性，提升机器人策略学习的泛化性能。

Abstract: Robots exhibit a rich variety of symmetries arising from their mechanical structure and the properties of their tasks. Although many robotics problems exhibit several symmetries simultaneously, existing approaches typically treat them in isolation, failing to exploit their combined potential. This paper introduces cross-space symmetry compositions, a framework for learning robot policies that are jointly equivariant to multiple symmetries across configuration and task spaces. Leveraging the differential-geometric structure of the forward kinematics map, we both descend symmetries from configuration to task space and lift symmetries from task to configuration space, enabling their composition within a unified representation space. We validate our framework on simulated and real-world experiments on a dual-arm robot, demonstrating that jointly leveraging multiple symmetries yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [202] [Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22748)
*Ismail Geles,Leonard Bauersfeld,Markus Wulfmeier,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明了多智能体强化学习（MARL）能为现实世界交互提供必要的安全支撑，以高速四旋翼无人机竞速为测试平台，通过联赛自对弈训练智能体掌握复杂气动交互和策略机动，在多人竞速中以超过22 m/s的速度击败人类冠军级飞行员，同时相比最先进的单智能体基线将碰撞率降低50%，且能零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在孤立或模拟环境中已达到超人类性能，但在共享动态现实空间中仍然脆弱。这种失败源于物理应用中主导的单智能体范式——将其他参与者忽略或视为环境噪声，阻碍了有效协调。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习框架，以高速四旋翼无人机竞速为高保真测试平台，通过联赛式自对弈训练智能体处理可变数量的竞速者，学习复杂气动交互和策略机动（包括主动防撞、超车、处理多智能体物理交互如下洗气流等）。

Result: 智能体在多人竞速中以超过22 m/s的速度击败人类冠军级飞行员，同时相比最先进的单智能体基线将碰撞率降低50%，且训练中使用多样化人工智能体能零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互中。

Conclusion: 实现鲁棒机器人共存的关键不在于孤立的约束，而在于多智能体交互的严格要求。

Abstract: Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl

</details>


### [203] [GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22812)
*Wenxuan Guo,Ziyuan Li,Meng Zhang,Yichen Liu,Yimeng Dong,Chuxi Xu,Yunfei Wei,Ze Chen,Erjin Zhou,Jianjiang Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GesVLA模型，通过将手势作为并行指令模态编码到潜空间，采用双VLM架构实现手势表示与动作策略的紧密耦合，并构建可扩展的手势数据生成流程，有效改善了复杂场景下机器人操作的空间歧义问题，实验证明手势的加入提高了目标定位准确率和人机交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖文本指令，在包含多个相似物体的复杂场景中难以解决空间歧义问题。

Method: 将手势特征编码到潜空间参与高层推理和低层动作生成；采用双VLM架构实现手势表示与动作策略的紧耦合；通过将手部模型渲染到真实场景图像上构建可扩展的手势数据生成流程；采用两阶段训练策略。

Result: 在多个真实机器人任务（积木操作、产品/农产品选择等）上，手势的加入持续提升了目标定位准确率和人机交互效率，尤其在复杂杂乱环境中效果显著。

Conclusion: 手势作为并行指令模态能有效增强VLA模型在复杂场景中的空间理解与操作能力，提升人机交互的自然性与效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.

</details>


### [204] [AwareVLN: Reasoning with Self-awareness for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22816)
*Wenxuan Guo,Xiuwei Xu,Yichen Liu,Xiangyu Li,Hang Yin,Huangxing Chen,Wenzhao Zheng,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AwareVLN通过引入结构化的自我推理机制，使VLN模型在完全端到端的方式下理解智能体状态和任务进度，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法要么依赖VLM进行端到端动作预测但缺乏显式的可解释性，要么依赖3D传感器构建场景地图进行启发式规划但阻碍了大规模视觉-语言预训练。需要一种既能保持端到端学习优势又具备自我感知能力的方法。

Method: (1) 结构化推理模块：培养空间和任务导向的自我感知能力；(2) 自动数据引擎：通过进度划分实现有效训练。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器的多个数据集上，AwareVLN显著优于之前的SOTA视觉-语言导航方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入自我感知推理机制，可以在不依赖额外3D传感器和大规模预训练的前提下，实现端到端、数据驱动的可解释导航，且性能领先。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to ground language instructions to its own movement within a visual environment. While state-of-the-art methods leverage the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end action prediction, they often lack an explicit and explainable understanding of the relationships between the agent, the instruction, and the scene. Conversely, explicitly building a scene map for heuristic planning is intuitively appealing but relies on additional 3D sensors and hinders large-scale vision-language pre-training. To bridge this gap, we propose AwareVLN, a novel framework that equips the navigation model with a self-aware reasoning mechanism, enabling it to understand the agent's state and task progress in a fully end-to-end and data-driven manner. Our approach features two key innovations: (1) a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, and (2) an automatic data engine with progress division for effective training. Extensive experiments on various datasets in Habitat simulator show our AwareVLN significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MOOD基准，用于评估大语言模型监控管线能否检测分布外（OOD）的对齐失败。研究发现守卫模型（安全分类器）在OOD场景下泛化能力差，而结合守卫模型与马氏距离及基于困惑度的OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%，且该组合的收益优于将守卫模型参数扩大20倍。作者主张OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多LLM的安全与对齐失败源于分布外（OOD）情景，即模型开发者未预料到的异常提示或响应模式。现有研究缺乏系统评估LLM监控管线能否检测这些OOD对齐失败的基准。

Method: 构建MOOD基准，包含受限训练集（用于训练监控器）和七个分布外测试集（涵盖多样化的对齐失败）。测试了守卫模型与四种OOD检测器（如马氏距离、基于困惑度的检测器）的组合方案。

Result: 守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化能力差；结合守卫模型与马氏距离及困惑度OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%；该组合的召回率增益优于将守卫模型参数扩大20倍的效果。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组件，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [206] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents是一个多智能体AI框架，将自然语言设计意图与迭代拓扑优化连接起来，通过视觉-语言推理和多轮修订，实现从设计意图到可制造原型的端到端生成，在60%的试验中生成符合审美偏好的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化中，设计师必须手动将定性设计意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转化为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器参数，缺乏自动化连接自然语言意图与优化过程的桥梁。

Method: 提出TO-Agents多智能体框架，包含：将自然语言问题描述转化为验证后的求解器输入、运行拓扑优化求解器、渲染3D拓扑结果、利用多视图视觉-语言推理结合独立评判智能体对结果进行批评并修订求解器参数，以及制造智能体后处理优化设计以适配增材制造。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准测试和手机支架产品设计两个长周期任务上，TO-Agents在60%的试验中至少生成一个符合审美偏好（自然树状分层结构）的设计，比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管线成功率高6倍。评判分数和人工评估表明该管线能识别有效参数杠杆、从糟糕修订中恢复并扩展设计探索空间。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的拓扑优化可以将设计师从低层级参数调优转向更高层级的形式与功能规格说明，同时需要为可靠的自主工程设计建立安全机制以应对过冲、选择性记忆、工具误用和参数推理错误等故障模式。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [207] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对USC Shoah Foundation和Yale Fortunoff Video Archive超过1600份大屠杀幸存者证词的计算分析，量化了两种口述历史档案的结构化程度。结果显示两者确实存在前人所述的风格差异，但也发现了显著的重叠，挑战了简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二元划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究领域长期认为USC证词（结构化、访谈者主导）与Yale证词（自由形式、开放式）存在根本性风格差异，这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案建设。作者希望用大规模计算方法批判性地检验这一经典论断。

Method: 对1600+份证词进行话语分割、主题建模和基于大语言模型的分析，通过主题连贯性、访谈者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布来量化"结构化程度"。

Result: 总体上证实了前人研究中发现的两种档案的结构差异，但同时揭示了两个档案间存在显著重叠，无论是在个体访谈内部还是跨共同叙事模式层面。

Conclusion: 简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二元分类不足以描述这些口述历史档案；研究提供了一个可扩展、可复制的比较语料库分析框架，对数字口述史、叙事分析以及公民科学标注平台设计有广泛启示。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [208] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom是一个通过组合式思维模式工程来合成前沿推理数据的框架。它先将困难问题的已验证解决方案分解为思维模式链，再训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容思维模式匹配，然后通过迭代应用检索到的思维模式组合新问题，最后用基于rollout的评判阶段标注问题难度并生成正确答案。在涵盖5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务的9个基准测试上，微调后的模型优于基线和基线蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理数据合成方法对控制问题难度的结构性因素可见性有限，导致多样性狭窄且难度控制不稳定。作者试图通过将推理问题难度视为原子化知识-推理转换（思维模式）的累积来克服这一局限。

Method: 1) 将困难问题的已验证解决方案分解为思维模式链；2) 训练检索模型匹配问题状态到兼容思维模式；3) 迭代应用检索到的思维模式到种子问题，结合分布对齐采样合成新问题；4) 基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并生成经过验证的正确答案。

Result: 在9个基准（涵盖5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务）上，基于多种模型系列和大小的微调结果优于基线和外部数据方法。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献，进一步分析表明MindLoom覆盖了广泛的推理模式并保持了有效的难度控制。

Conclusion: MindLoom通过组合式思维模式工程能有效合成高质量、多样性好且难度可控的前沿推理数据，显著提升LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [209] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，一个证据模型原型，旨在通过数据模型扩展和现代化改造，解决AOP-Wiki在支持AOP持续增长和演化方面的局限性，为计算生成AOP、定量AOP（qAOP）奠定基础，并提升AOP框架与新方法学（NAMs）的整合，以服务于下一代风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOP-Wiki作为全球AOP知识库，其现有数据模型和应用基础设施存在局限性，无法支持AOP领域的持续增长和演化。同时，生成式AI的变革性能力为AOP-Wiki数据现代化提供了新机遇，亟需利用AOP核心原则来指导AI在AOP相关信息聚合与结构化中的应用。

Method: 提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0证据模型原型，具体通过三个方向实现数据模型扩展：（1）AOP-Wiki内部质量改进；（2）证据结构化以增强AOP的FAIR原则和AI就绪性；（3）改进AOP框架与NAMs的整合，以服务下一代风险评估。

Result: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0作为第三个证据模型原型，具体展示了数据模型扩展方案，以及将AOP-Wiki转型以更好服务监管科学、生物医学和One Health等新兴应用场景的愿景。

Conclusion: 通过AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0的提出，为支持计算生成AOP和定量AOP奠定了基础，有望推动AOP知识库的现代化改造，进而更好地服务于监管科学和AOP在生物医学及One Health领域的新兴应用。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [210] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过询问AI系统不合理类别成员（如“橄榄是交通工具吗？”）来探究概念边界，发现AI与人类在概念理解上存在显著差异（如将“词语”归为“交通工具”），并展示了这些差异如何导致有害的下游行为，影响AI安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过询问合理类别成员（如“汽车是交通工具吗？”）来探测概念理解，但这种方法容易触发模型训练数据中的模式记忆。作者提出一种替代策略，通过询问不合理类别成员来刻画概念边界，从而更准确地评估AI系统是否具备人类级别的概念理解能力，这是构建安全可靠的AI系统的关键步骤。

Method: 选取Rosch和Mervis经典心理学研究中的基本概念和对象，让AI系统完成两类任务：(1) 将对象分配到正确的上位类别；(2) 将同一对象分配到不匹配的上位类别。然后将AI的分配结果与人类参与者的结果进行比较，覆盖了类别内和跨类别分配任务的全部范围。

Result: 发现AI模型在多个概念上与人类存在显著且有意义的差异，包括：将“词语”归为“交通工具”和“服装”等类别；将部分“蔬菜”类别成员识别为“水果”；将非武器类别的示例归入“武器”类别。

Conclusion: 这些概念错位实例不仅揭示了AI与人类概念理解的根本差异，而且会转化为有问题的下游行为，对AI安全性产生实际影响。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [211] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，过度依赖AI辅助会削弱逻辑推理技能的培养，而AI的信息丰富程度（低信息vs高信息）对学习效果有显著调节作用：低信息AI既不能提升即时表现，也会损害长期技能发展；高信息AI能提升短期表现，但对长期技能发展的影响具有异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助在人类问题解决中的使用频率和信息丰富度对个体技能发展的影响，澄清AI是补充还是削弱人类推理能力。

Method: 在受控的逻辑推理任务中设置按需AI辅助，通过比较重度/轻度AI使用者以及不同信息丰富度（低信息/高信息）的AI辅助效果，分析技能发展的差异。

Result: 重度AI使用者技能发展更弱；低信息AI既不提升即时表现，也损害长期表现；高信息AI提升短期表现，但长期效果异质。

Conclusion: AI对技能发展的影响取决于使用情境：高信息AI可补充独立推理能力，低信息AI则起替代作用；需要合理调控AI的访问和使用以促进技能发展。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [212] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了拒绝压制问题，将其形式化为针对线性探针的潜空间规避攻击，揭示了现有方法本质上是将表示投影到决策边界上（即最小置信度规避攻击），并提出一种受控潜空间规避攻击（CLE），通过将表示投影越过边界并优化置信度，在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝压制方法虽然经验成功，但缺乏对潜空间变换及其为何能压制拒绝行为的原理性解释。

Method: 将拒绝压制重新定义为针对线性探针的潜空间规避攻击，指出前人工作的差异均值方向天然定义了这样一个探针，其消融操作恰好是到决策边界的投影（最小置信度规避攻击）。在此基础上提出受控潜空间规避攻击（CLE），将表示投影越过决策边界进入合规区域，并优化置信度。

Result: CLE在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率，优于现有拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 拒绝压制本质上是一种潜空间规避攻击，但仅到达决策边界不足以有效压制拒绝行为，需要将表示进一步推入合规区域才能实现更好的攻击效果。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [213] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench是一个基于200个真实多轮人机对话的情感智能基准，通过逐轮标注评估模型的情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量。实验发现这些能力相互独立，且偏好对齐和响应质量比情感标签准确性更具区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准依赖合成提示、单轮案例或第三方标注，无法直接衡量模型在真实对话过程中推断并回应用户情绪状态的能力。

Method: 基于200个真实多轮人机对话，参与者与匿名LLM对话并提供逐轮情感状态、模型行为和偏好回应标注，评估11个模型在情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量四个维度上的表现。

Result: 模型在不同能力维度上的排名大体独立，表明情感智能行为可分解为可分离的能力；偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确性更具模型区分度。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为需要预测特定用户在特定上下文中期望的回应类型，这一区分会被聚合评分掩盖，且单轮或合成格式无法直接捕捉跨轮次的能力差异。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [214] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示AI平台对话日志测量的职业暴露度存在偏差：其更多反映平台用户结构而非真实劳动力市场，且在不同设置下估计结果差异巨大（系数变化1.9倍、符号相反等）。通过重新加权至BLS劳动力份额可大幅削弱估计值（42-93%），并形式化了非经典测量误差，发现偏差低估了替代效应甚于增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献大量使用AI平台对话日志来测量职业暴露度，但该方法可能因平台用户结构偏差而无法准确反映真实劳动力市场，需系统检验其测量有效性和稳健性。

Method: 固定结果、样本、控制变量和估计量，仅改变平台输入（同厂商内消费者vs企业渠道），比较ChatGPT后就业弹性系数变化；使用BLS劳动力份额进行重新加权；形式化非经典测量误差模型并推导概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 仅改变平台输入导致系数变化1.9倍；消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反；BLS重新加权使估计值衰减42%-93%；测量误差导致替代效应被低估的程度大于增强效应。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在系统性偏差，需谨慎使用并考虑劳动力人口权重校正，否则将严重扭曲对AI就业影响的估计。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [215] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合因果发现与双极论证框架(BAF)的新方法，通过半稳定语义找出特征扩展集，解释模型为何做出特定预测，在两个基准数据集上验证并对比了传统事后可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法虽能识别哪些特征与模型预测相关，但无法阐明为何做出特定决策，缺乏对预测背后因果推理机制的解释能力。

Method: 首先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，然后将这些关系转化为双极论证框架(BAF)以表示特征间的支持与反对交互，最后利用半稳定语义(semi-stable semantics)寻找特征扩展集来解释特定预测结果的原因。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上进行了实验验证，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该方法能够提供超越传统相关性分析的因果级解释，通过论证推理明确说明模型为何做出特定预测，填补了XAI在"why"层面的解释空白。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [216] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI谄媚行为缺乏统一定义的问题，通过综述70篇论文构建了行为分类体系（区分谄媚对象是用户观点/信念还是个人特质/情感，以及表达方式是显性直接还是隐性间接），并调查106位专家发现虽有94.3%认为该问题严重，但对具体哪些行为属于谄媚存在显著分歧。研究表明AI谄媚是一系列行为家族，需要不同的测量、干预和治理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI谄媚行为缺乏统一定义，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统虽能抵抗某种谄媚但会表现出其他形式，阻碍了该领域的研究进展和有效治理。

Method: （1）综述70篇相关论文，构建AI谄媚行为的分类体系；（2）调查106位AI谄媚及相关领域专家，评估研究者对具体行为是否属于谄媚的一致程度。

Result: （1）分类体系区分了两个维度：谄媚对象（用户观点/信念 vs. 个人特质/情感）和表达方式（显性直接 vs. 隐性间接）；现有研究主要集中在显性信念导向的谄媚，忽视了更隐蔽的、面向个人的行为。（2）94.3%专家认为谄媚是AI系统的严重问题，但对具体哪些行为属于谄媚存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚是一个广泛的行为家族，包含不同的测量挑战、干预需求和治理含义；所提出的分类体系为该领域的理解和应对提供了共享词汇。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [217] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL，一种从人群偏好中提取安全对齐技能并组合以安全完成下游任务的层次化框架，在不使用显式安全奖励的情况下显著降低安全成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法虽然能从偏好中揭示隐性安全目标，但直接优化偏好学习奖励模型与下游任务奖励的组合存在固有限制，需要一种更有效的方法从人群偏好中提取共享安全准则并将其迁移到下游任务中。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL层次化框架：从人群偏好数据中提取安全对齐的技能（skills），然后通过高层策略将这些技能组合起来，安全地解决下游任务。

Result: 在安全RL环境和初步LLM风格任务中，该方法在不使用显式安全奖励的情况下显著降低了安全成本，任务性能与使用真实安全信号训练的oracle方法相当。

Conclusion: 从人群偏好中提取共享安全准则并作为技能迁移到下游任务是有效的，避免了直接奖励组合的缺陷，为无显式安全信号下的安全RL提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [218] [Toward AI VIS Co-Scientists: A General and End-to-End Agent Harness for Solving Complex Data Visualization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21825)
*Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Kuangshi Ai,Kaiyuan Tang,Chaoli Wang,Peer-Timo Bremer,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的智能体框架（VIS co-scientist），仅基于数据和高级任务描述，即可自主设计并构建定制化的可视化分析应用（VIS App）。该系统通过多个智能体协调完成探索性分析、规划、环境配置、实现、界面验证和任务完成度评估，并在多个IEEE SciVis竞赛数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据分析与可视化需要跨领域专业知识（数据管理、分析、可视化设计与实现），对非专业领域的研究人员门槛过高。现有方法缺乏能根据高层任务描述自主完成长周期、端到端可视化分析工作的智能系统。

Method: 提出VIS co-scientist智能体框架，包含多个协作智能体：探索性分析、规划、环境配置、实现、界面验证和任务完成度评估。每个阶段生成文档和指令工件，指导下游工作并支持迭代优化。系统仅需输入数据和高级任务描述，自主输出功能完整的单页VIS App。

Result: 在多个IEEE SciVis竞赛数据集上验证，系统能自主生成具有已验证的联动视图行为的、高度定制化的单页VIS App，满足领域专家指定的任务需求。

Conclusion: 该工作向通用AI联合科学家（AI co-scientist）愿景迈出了重要一步，展示了自主执行长周期可视化分析任务的可行性，智能体框架可有效将高层任务描述转化为可交互的可视化分析应用。

Abstract: The ability to inspect, interpret, and communicate complex data is crucial for virtually any scientific endeavor, but often requires significant expertise outside the core domain ranging from data management and analysis to visualization design and implementation. We present an end-to-end agentic harness that, based on only the data and a high level description of the tasks, independently designs custom visual analysis applications (VIS apps). This represents an important step towards a general AI co-scientist envisioned by many as an autonomous system that can autonomously execute long horizon tasks based on high-level directions. Our proposed VIS co-scientist is an essential component of this broader AI co-scientist vision: a harness that can autonomously analyze data and design visualization solutions using a collection of agents and specialized skills that coordinate exploratory analysis, plan, configure the environment, implement, validate the interface, and most importantly evaluate the overall task completion. Each stage produces document and instruction artifacts that guide downstream work and enable iterative refinement. We validate this approach on IEEE SciVis Contests spanning multiple science and engineering fields. These contests serve as ideal proving grounds because they encode real-world complexity: ambiguous requirements, diverse data modalities, design trade-offs, and task-driven validation. Given only the data and target tasks, our system autonomously produces functional single-page VIS Apps with verified linked-view behavior, highly customized to domain experts' specified tasks and needs.

</details>


### [219] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLUID是首个在生产规模的直播推荐系统中完全摒弃候选侧物品ID的框架。它通过跨领域多模态编码器（联合训练短视频和直播生成离散层次化编码LUCID），结合晚期融合、无ID的设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入，并通过分阶段预热和在线增量训练稳定模型。部署在超十亿用户的工业直播推荐系统中，实现了观看时长+0.55%、冷启房间浏览量+2.05%、活跃时长+0.05%的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖ID协同过滤，但在直播推荐中，直播间仅持续数十分钟，导致物品ID长期处于冷启动状态，ID-centric排序模型难以泛化。

Method: FLUID包含三个关键组件：(1) 跨领域多模态编码器，联合训练短视频和直播，生成离散层次化编码LUCID；(2) 晚期融合、无ID设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入；(3) 分阶段预热策略，配合在线增量训练稳定模型。

Result: 在超十亿用户的工业部署中，FLUID带来Quality Watch Duration提升0.55%，Cold-Start Room Views提升2.05%，Active Hours提升0.05%。
